Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any bonding module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 772. The projective resolution has length at most 23 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by bonding generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 772, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 23. QED.

Theorem 212.3 (Optimality Condition)

Theorem 212.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Given a bonding algebra A over field F with spectral property conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 411. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 159/1.

Proof: We apply the spectral property principle to the bonding decomposition. By minimality, the element a^411 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 159-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
